**«PUBLISH HANDLE BUILT — was blocked on the release, now CLEARED» (2026-07-26 ~01:3xZ; Waffles handed it to me when Athena hit context depth). FRAME branch `artemis/fact-publisher` off `feat/argus-v1-route-seam`, pushed, worktree `frame/.worktrees/publish-handle`. c3ce874 RED (4 walls + const assertion pinning Clone+Send+Sync) → a477414 GREEN. DESIGN (Athena's, correct in every particular): publish capability comes from Announcer's inner `Arc<Shared>`, NOT the struct — `intake_closed` + `truth` MOVED into `Shared` (both already interior-mutable), `FactPublisher` derives Clone over that Arc, `stop(self)` still consumes the Announcer, and **`announce_fact` now DELEGATES to `publisher().publish_fact()`** so app path and announcer path are provably the same bytes. DEAD ENDS RECORDED (don't re-walk): `Arc<Announcer>` is defeated by `stop(self)`; `std::sync::mpsc::Sender` is Send but NOT Sync (AppRouteHandler = `Arc<dyn Fn(Vec<u8>)->AppRouteReply + Send + Sync>`). Full frame-host suite green, clippy -D warnings + fmt clean. **MY SIMPLIFICATION: the `AppSpec.on_publisher` hook she scoped is NOT NEEDED — `FactAnnouncement` ALREADY receives `&Announcer`, so the app's existing `announce` closure calls `.publisher()` and stashes it. Ordering VERIFIED not assumed: `announce` runs inside `Application::boot` via `wire_bus_participants`, strictly before the page server binds, so no route can fire before the handle is stashed. Kills the 8-construction-site churn and the breaking-shaped MINOR she'd flagged.** ⛔ **BLOCKER (raised to Waffles, needs TOM): argus pins `frame-host = { version = "=0.4.0" }` from crates.io under a manifest stating it builds with no Frame checkout present — so the app CANNOT reach FactPublisher until frame-host is RELEASED. BOTH ENDS of the shared vector are now asserted and the cross-language agreement held on the FIRST run (Rust decode byte-identical to the event authored from the TS side). Rules landed as rules: `at` injected not read inside (a now() would make the vector unstable by construction); blank/missing session_id → "unknown" + malformed flag, NEVER dropped; unnamed session travels as JSON null not "" so the page distinguishes no-name from empty-name; string/float/negative number = typed BadField, never coerced; every refusal names its contract path. `the_capture_still_carries_no_branch_or_dirty` now exists on BOTH sides as a self-announcing tripwire for lane 2.
